[ASSISTANT]
Good progress (4.61M). Now the memory read mux is critical. Let me restructure the corner-turn memory for shallower muxes and narrower width.


  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  -- full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n// Row pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered corner turn -> column\n// pass (OUT_SHIFT=15).  Bit-exact reproduction of the fixed point\n// behaviour of the original core_jpeg implementation.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct1d #(\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 32\n)(\n    input  wire                       clk,\n    input  wire                       rst,\n    input  wire                       valid_i,\n    input  wire [2:0]                 idx_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d0_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d1_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d2_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d3_i,\n    output wire                       valid_o,\n    output wire [5:0]                 cnt_o,\n    output wire signed [OUT_W-1:0]    data_o\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C1 = 16'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C2 = 16'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C3 = 16'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C4 = 16'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C5 = 16'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C6 = 16'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C7 = 16'sd799;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// even part pre-adds (latched on beats 0 and 2)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire signed [IN_W:0] i0_sum = d0_i + d2_i;\nwire signed [IN_W:0] i0_dif = d0_i - d2_i;\n\nreg signed [IN_W:0] i0q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    i0q <= 0;\nelse if (idx_i == 3'd0)\n    i0q <= i0_sum;\nelse if (idx_i == 3'd2)\n    i0q <= i0_dif;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// multiplier operand select\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [IN_W-1:0] a0,a1,a2,a3;\nreg signed [IN_W:0]   a4;\nreg signed [15:0]     b0,b1,b2,b3,b4;\n\nalways @* begin\n    a0 = d1_i; b0 = C2;\n    a1 = d3_i; b1 = C6;\n    a2 = d2_i; b2 = C5;\n    a3 = d1_i; b3 = C3;\n    a4 = i0q;  b4 = C4;\n    case (idx_i)\n    3'd1: begin\n        a0 = d0_i; b0 = C1;\n        a1 = d3_i; b1 = C7;\n    end\n    3'd3, 3'd4: begin\n        a0 = d0_i; b0 = C7;\n        a1 = d3_i; b1 = C1;\n        a2 = d2_i; b2 = C3;\n        a3 = d1_i; b3 = C5;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        a0 = d1_i; b0 = C6;\n        a1 = d3_i; b1 = C2;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] p0,p1,p2,p3,p4;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    p0 <= 32'sd0; p1 <= 32'sd0; p2 <= 32'sd0; p3 <= 32'sd0; p4 <= 32'sd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    p0 <= a0 * b0;\n    p1 <= a1 * b1;\n    p2 <= a2 * b2;\n    p3 <= a3 * b3;\n    p4 <= a4 * b4;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// pipeline index / valid tracking\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [2:0] idx_d1, idx_d2;\nreg       vld_d1, vld_d2;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    idx_d1 <= 3'd0; idx_d2 <= 3'd0; vld_d1 <= 1'b0; vld_d2 <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse begin\n    idx_d1 <= idx_i;  vld_d1 <= valid_i;\n    idx_d2 <= idx_d1; vld_d2 <= vld_d1;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// butterfly accumulators\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5,o_s6,o_s7,o_t0,o_t1,o_t2,o_t3,o_t4,o_t5,o_t6,o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5,o_t5_6;\nreg signed [31:0] sp5,sp6;\n\n// (x*181)/256 with signed truncation toward zero\nwire signed [31:0] sb5 = sp5 + (sp5[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\nwire signed [31:0] sb6 = sp6 + (sp6[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5<=0; o_s6<=0; o_s7<=0; o_t0<=0; o_t1<=0; o_t2<=0; o_t3<=0;\n    o_t4<=0; o_t5<=0; o_t6<=0; o_t7<=0; o_t6_5<=0; o_t5_6<=0;\n    sp5<=0; sp6<=0;\nend\nelse begin\n    case (idx_d1)\n    3'd0: o_t3 <= p0 + p1;\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= p0 + p1;\n        o_s6 <= p2 + p3;\n        o_t0 <= p4;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3;\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3;\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3: o_t4 <= (p0 - p1) + (p2 - p3);\n    3'd4: begin\n        o_t0 <= p0 - p1;\n        o_s5 <= p2 - p3;\n        o_t5 <= (p0 - p1) - (p2 - p3);\n        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        o_t3   <= p0 - p1;\n        o_t4   <= p4;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    3'd6: begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        sp5  <= o_t6_5 * 181;\n        sp6  <= o_t5_6 * 181;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= sb5 >>> 8;\n        o_s6 <= sb6 >>> 8;\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row output buffer / output streamer\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [OUT_W-1:0] blk [0:7];\nreg signed [31:0]      blk_tmp;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    blk[0]<=0; blk[1]<=0; blk[2]<=0; blk[3]<=0;\n    blk[4]<=0; blk[5]<=0; blk[6]<=0; blk[7]<=0;\n    blk_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (vld_d2) begin\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd3) begin\n        blk[0]  <= (o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk_tmp <= (o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[3]  <= (o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[4]  <= (o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    end\n\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd6)\n        blk[7] <= blk_tmp;\n\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd7) begin\n        blk[2] <= (o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[5] <= (o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[1] <= (o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[6] <= (o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] vq;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    vq <= 8'd0;\nelse\n    vq <= {vq[6:0], vld_d2};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign valid_o = vq[6];\nassign cnt_o   = ptr_q;\nassign data_o  = blk[ptr_q[2:0]];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// top level\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam integer MW = 20;      // corner-turn word width\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row pass\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire                 x_valid;\nwire [5:0]           x_cnt;\nwire signed [MW-1:0] x_data;\n\nidct1d #(.IN_W(16), .OUT_SHIFT(11), .OUT_W(MW)) u_x\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.d0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.d1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.d2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.d3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.valid_o(x_valid)\n    ,.cnt_o(x_cnt)\n    ,.data_o(x_data)\n);\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// corner turn memory:  tmem[row][{bank,col}]  (transposed store)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [MW-1:0] tmem [0:7][0:15];\n\nreg        wr_blk;\nreg [1:0]  blk_rdy;\nreg        rd_blk;\nreg        rd_act;\nreg [5:0]  rd_cnt;\n\nwire [2:0] wr_row = x_cnt[5:3];\nwire [2:0] wr_col = x_cnt[2:0];\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (x_valid)\n    tmem[wr_row][{wr_blk,wr_col}] <= x_data;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// column staging registers (break memory mux -> multiplier path)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [MW-1:0] stage [0:7];\n\nwire        rd_valid = rd_act;\nwire [2:0]  rd_beat  = rd_cnt[2:0];\nwire [2:0]  rd_col   = rd_cnt[5:3];\nwire [2:0]  ld_col   = rd_act ? (rd_col + 3'd1) : 3'd0;\nwire [3:0]  rd_addr  = {rd_blk, rd_col};\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rd_act ? stage[4] <= tmem[3'd4][rd_addr];\n    stage[5] <= tmem[3'd5][rd_addr];\n    stage[6] <= tmem[3'd6][rd_addr];\n    stage[7] <= @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    wr_blk  <= 1'b0;\n    blk_rdy <= 2'b0;\n    rd_blk  <= 1'b0;\n    rd_act  <= 1'b0;\n    rd_cnt  <= 6'd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    if (x_valid && x_cnt == 6'd63) Now let me restructure with explicit constant multiplies (so Yosys builds shift-add networks instead of general multipliers) and replace the parity mux with per-beat lane registers.




  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  -- full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n// Row pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered corner turn -> column\n// pass (OUT_SHIFT=15).  Bit-exact reproduction of the fixed point\n// behaviour of the original core_jpeg implementation.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct1d #(\n    parameter @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    i0q <= 0;\nelse if (idx_i == 3'd0)\n    i0q <= i0_sum;\nelse if (idx_i == 3'd2)\n    i0q <= i0_dif;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// multiplier products -- every operand pair is a signal times a\n// compile time constant so that only shift/add networks are built.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] p0,p1,p2,p3,p4;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    p0 <= 32'sd0; p1 <= 32'sd0; p2 <= 32'sd0; p3 <= 32'sd0; p4 <= 32'sd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    // even beats {0,2,5,7}\n    p0 <= d1_i * C2;\n    p1 <= d3_i * C6;\n    p2 <= d2_i * C5;\n    p3 <= d1_i * C3;\n    p4 <= i0q  * C4;\n    case (idx_i)\n    3'd1: begin                      // odd set\n        p0 <= d0_i * C1;\n        p1 <= d3_i * C7;\n    end\n    3'd3, 3'd4, 3'd6: begin          // odd set\n        p0 <= d0_i * C7;\n + (sp5[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\nwire signed [31:0] sb6 = sp6 + (sp6[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5<=0; o_s6<=0; o_s7<=0; o_t0<=0; o_t1<=0; o_t2<=0;    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam integer MW = 20;      // corner-turn word width\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row pass\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire                 x_valid;\nwire [5:0]           x_cnt;\nwire signed [MW-1:0] x_data;\n\nidct1d #(.IN_W(16), .OUT_SHIFT(11), .OUT_W(MW)) u_x\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.d0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.d1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.d2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.d3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.valid_o(x_valid)\n    ,.cnt_o(x_cnt)\n    ,.data_o(x_data)\n);\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// corner turn memory:  tmem[row][{bank,col}]  (transposed store)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [MW-1:0] tmem [0:7][0:15];\n\nreg        wr_blk;\nreg [1:0]  blk_rdy;\nreg        rd_blk;\nreg        rd_act;\nreg [5:0]  rd_cnt;\n\nwire [2:0] wr_row = x_cnt[5:3];\nwire [2:0] wr_col = x_cnt[2:0];\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (x_valid)\n    tmem[wr_row][{wr_blk,wr_col}] <= Bit-exact reproduction of the fixed point\n// behaviour of the original core_jpeg implementation.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct1d #(\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 32\n)(\n    input  wire                       clk,\n    input  wire                       rst,\n    input  wire                       valid_i,\n    input  wire [2:0]                 idx_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d0_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d1_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d2_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d3_i,\n    output wire                       valid_o,\n    output wire [5:0]                 cnt_o,\n    output wire signed [OUT_W-1:0]    data_o\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C1 = 16'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C2 = 16'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C3 = 16'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C4 = 16'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C5 = 16'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C6 = 16'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C7 = 16'sd799;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// multiplier products. p3 <= 32'sd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    // even lanes: d0=c0 d1=c2 d2=c4 d3=c6\n    p0 <= d1_i * C2;\n    p1 <= d3_i * C6;\n    p2 <= d0_i * C4;\n    p3 <= d2_i * C4;\n    case (idx_i)\n    3'd1: begin                       // odd lanes: d0=c1 d1=c3 d2=c5 d3=c7\n        p0 <= d0_i * C1;\n        p1 <= d3_i * C7;\n        p2 <= d2_i * C5;\n        p3 <= d1_i * C3;\n    end\n    3'd3, 3'd4, 3'd6: begin\n        p0 <= d0_i * C7;\n        p1 <= d3_i * C1;\n        p2 <= d2_i * C3;\n        p3 <= d1_i * C5;\n    end\n    3'd5, 3'd7: begin\n        p0 <= d1_i * C6;\n        p1 <= d3_i * C2;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// pipeline index / valid tracking\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [2:0] idx_d1, idx_d2;\nreg       vld_d1, vld_d2;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    idx_d1 <= 3'd0; idx_d2 <= 3'd0; vld_d1 <= 1'b0; vld_d2 <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse begin\n    idx_d1 <= idx_i;  vld_d1 <= valid_i;\n    idx_d2 <= idx_d1; vld_d2 <= vld_d1;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// butterfly accumulators\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5,o_s6,o_s7,o_t0,o_t1,o_t2,o_t3,o_t4,o_t5,o_t6,o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5,o_t5_6;\nreg signed [31:0] sp5,sp6;\n\n// (x*181)/256 with signed truncation toward zero\nwire signed [31:0] sb5 = sp5 + (sp5[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\nwire signed [31:0] sb6 = sp6 + (sp6[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5<=0; o_s6<=0; o_s7<=0; o_t0<=0; o_t1<=0; o_t2<=0; o_t3<=0;\n    o_t4<=0; o_t5<=0; o_t6<=0; o_t7<=0; o_t6_5<=0; o_t5_6<=0;\n    sp5<=0; sp6<=0;\nend\nelse begin\n    case (idx_d1)\n    3'd0: begin\n        o_t3 <= p0 + p1;                // s3\n        o_t0 <= p2 + p3;                // s0\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= p0 + p1;\n        o_s6 <= p2 + p3;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3;            // t0\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3;            // t3\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3: o_t4 <= (p0 - p1) + (p2 - p3);\n    3'd4: begin\n        o_t0 <= p0 - p1;                // s4\n        o_s5 <= p2 - p3;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        o_t3   <= p0 - p1;              // s2\n        o_t4   <= p2 - p3;              // s1\n        o_t5   <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n        o_t6   <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row output buffer / output blk[0]<=0; blk[1]<=0; blk[2]<=0; blk[3]<=0;\n    blk[4]<=0; blk[5]<=0; blk[6]<=0; blk[7]<=0;\n    blk_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (vld_d2) begin\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd3) begin\n        blk[0]  <= (o_t0 + blk[ptr_q[2:0]];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// top level\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule width\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row pass\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire                 x_valid;\nwire [5:0]           x_cnt;\nwire signed [MW-1:0] x_data;\n\nidct1d #(.IN_W(16), .OUT_SHIFT(11), .OUT_W(MW)) u_x\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.d0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.d1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.d2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.d3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.valid_o(x_valid)\n    ,.cnt_o(x_cnt)\n    ,.data_o(x_data)\n);\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// corner turn memory:  tmem[row][{bank,col}]\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [MW-1:0] tmem [0:7][0:15];\n\nreg        wr_blk;\nreg [1:0]  blk_rdy;\nreg        rd_blk;\nreg        rd_act;\nreg [5:0]  rd_cnt;\n\nwire [2:0] wr_row = x_cnt[5:3];\nwire [2:0] wr_col = x_cnt[2:0];\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (x_valid)\n    tmem[wr_row][{wr_blk,wr_col}] <= 6'd63);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    wr_blk  <= 1'b0;\n    blk_rdy <= 2'b0;\n    rd_blk  <= 1'b0;\n    rd_act  <= 1'b0;\n    rd_cnt  <= 6'd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    if (x_valid && Best so far is design5 (4.33M). Let me try replacing the variable-constant multipliers with literal constant multiplies (shift-add networks instead of array multipliers).


  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  -- full 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n// Row pass (OUT_SHIFT=11) -> double-buffered corner turn -> column\n// pass (OUT_SHIFT=15).  Bit-exact reproduction of the fixed point\n// behaviour of the original core_jpeg implementation.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct1d #(\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 32\n)(\n    input  wire                       clk,\n    input  wire                       rst,\n    input  wire                       valid_i,\n    input  wire [2:0]                 idx_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d0_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d1_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d2_i,\n    input  wire signed [IN_W-1:0]     d3_i,\n    output wire                       valid_o,\n    output wire [5:0]                 cnt_o,\n    output wire signed [OUT_W-1:0]    data_o\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C1 = 16'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C2 = 16'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C3 = 16'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C4 = 16'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C5 = 16'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C6 = 16'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [15:0] C7 = 16'sd799;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// even part pre-adds (latched on beats 0 and 2)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire signed [IN_W:0] i0_sum = d0_i + d2_i;\nwire signed [IN_W:0] i0_dif = d0_i - d2_i;\n\nreg signed [IN_W:0] i0q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    i0q <= 0;\nelse if (idx_i == 3'd0)\n    i0q <= i0_sum;\nelse if (idx_i == 3'd2)\n    i0q <= i0_dif;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// multiplier products.  Every term is a signal times a LITERAL\n// constant so Yosys builds cheap shift/add networks.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire odd_b  = (idx_i == 3'd1) | (idx_i == 3'd3) |\n              (idx_i == 3'd4) | (idx_i == 3'd6);\nwire ev_a   = (idx_i == 3'd0) | (idx_i == 3'd2);\n\nreg signed [31:0] p0,p1,p2,p3,p4;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    p0 <= 32'sd0; p1 <= 32'sd0; p2 <= 32'sd0; p3 <= 32'sd0; p4 <= 32'sd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    // lane 0 : operand d0 (odd beats) / d1 (even beats)\n    if (idx_i == 3'd1)          p0 <= d0_i * C1;\n    else if (ev_a)              p0 <= d1_i * C2;\n    else if (idx_i == 3'd5 ||\n             idx_i == 3'd7)     p0 <= d1_i * C6;\n    else                        p0 <= d0_i * C7;\n\n    // lane 1 : operand d3\n    if (idx_i == 3'd1)          p1 <= d3_i * C7;\n    else if (ev_a)              p1 <= d3_i * C6;\n    else if (idx_i == 3'd5 ||\n             idx_i == 3'd7)     p1 <= d3_i * C2;\n    else                        p1 <= d3_i * C1;\n\n    // lane 2 : operand d2\n    if (odd_b)                  p2 <= d2_i * C3;\n    else                        p2 <= d2_i * C5;\n\n    // lane 3 : operand d1\n    if (odd_b)                  p3 <= d1_i * C5;\n    else                        p3 <= d1_i * C3;\n\n    // lane 4 : even part (c0 +- c4) * C4\n    p4 <= i0q * C4;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// pipeline index / valid tracking\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [2:0] idx_d1, idx_d2;\nreg       vld_d1, vld_d2;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    idx_d1 <= 3'd0; idx_d2 <= 3'd0; vld_d1 <= 1'b0; vld_d2 <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse begin\n    idx_d1 <= idx_i;  vld_d1 <= valid_i;\n    idx_d2 <= idx_d1; vld_d2 <= vld_d1;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// butterfly accumulators\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5,o_s6,o_s7,o_t0,o_t1,o_t2,o_t3,o_t4,o_t5,o_t6,o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5,o_t5_6;\nreg signed [31:0] sp5,sp6;\n\n// (x*181)/256 with signed truncation toward zero\nwire signed [31:0] sb5 = sp5 + (sp5[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\nwire signed [31:0] sb6 = sp6 + (sp6[31] ? 32'sd255 : 32'sd0);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5<=0; o_s6<=0; o_s7<=0; o_t0<=0; o_t1<=0; o_t2<=0; o_t3<=0;\n    o_t4<=0; o_t5<=0; o_t6<=0; o_t7<=0; o_t6_5<=0; o_t5_6<=0;\n    sp5<=0; sp6<=0;\nend\nelse begin\n    case (idx_d1)\n    3'd0: o_t3 <= p0 + p1;\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= p0 + p1;\n        o_s6 <= p2 + p3;\n        o_t0 <= p4;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3;\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3;\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3: o_t4 <= (p0 - p1) + (p2 - p3);\n    3'd4: begin\n        o_t0 <= p0 - p1;\n        o_s5 <= p2 - p3;\n        o_t5 <= (p0 - p1) - (p2 - p3);\n        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        o_t3   <= p0 - p1;\n        o_t4   <= p4;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    3'd6: begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        sp5  <= o_t6_5 * 181;\n        sp6  <= o_t5_6 * 181;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= sb5 >>> 8;\n        o_s6 <= sb6 >>> 8;\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row output buffer / output streamer\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [OUT_W-1:0] blk [0:7];\nreg signed [31:0]      blk_tmp;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    blk[0]<=0; blk[1]<=0; blk[2]<=0; blk[3]<=0;\n    blk[4]<=0; blk[5]<=0; blk[6]<=0; blk[7]<=0;\n    blk_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (vld_d2) begin\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd3) begin\n        blk[0]  <= (o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk_tmp <= (o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[3]  <= (o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[4]  <= (o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    end\n\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd6)\n        blk[7] <= blk_tmp;\n\n    if (idx_d2 == 3'd7) begin\n        blk[2] <= (o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[5] <= (o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[1] <= (o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n        blk[6] <= (o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] vq;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    vq <= 8'd0;\nelse\n    vq <= {vq[6:0], vld_d2};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign valid_o = vq[6];\nassign cnt_o   = ptr_q;\nassign data_o  = blk[ptr_q[2:0]];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// top level\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam integer MW = 20;      // corner-turn word width\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row pass\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire                 x_valid;\nwire [5:0]           x_cnt;\nwire signed [MW-1:0] x_data;\n\nidct1d #(.IN_W(16), .OUT_SHIFT(11), .OUT_W(MW)) u_x\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.d0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.d1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.d2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.d3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.valid_o(x_valid)\n    ,.cnt_o(x_cnt)\n    ,.data_o(x_data)\n);\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// corner turn memory:  tmem[row][{bank,col}]\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [MW-1:0] tmem [0:7][0:15];\n\nreg        wr_blk;\nreg [1:0]  blk_rdy;\nreg        rd_blk;\nreg        rd_act;\nreg [5:0]  rd_cnt;\n\nwire [2:0] wr_row = x_cnt[5:3];\nwire [2:0] wr_col = x_cnt[2:0];\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (x_valid)\n    tmem[wr_row][{wr_blk,wr_col}] <= x_data;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// column staging registers (break memory mux -> multiplier path)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [MW-1:0] stage [0:7];\n\nwire        rd_valid = rd_act;\nwire [2:0]  rd_beat  = rd_cnt[2:0];\nwire [2:0]  rd_col   = rd_cnt[5:3];\nwire [2:0]  ld_col   = rd_act ? (rd_col + 3'd1) : 3'd0;\nwire [3:0]  rd_addr  = {rd_blk, ld_col};\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rd_act ? (rd_beat == 3'd7) : blk_rdy[rd_blk]) begin\n    stage[0] <= tmem[3'd0][rd_addr];\n    stage[1] <= tmem[3'd1][rd_addr];\n    stage[2] <= tmem[3'd2][rd_addr];\n    stage[3] <= tmem[3'd3][rd_addr];\n    stage[4] <= tmem[3'd4][rd_addr];\n    stage[5] <= tmem[3'd5][rd_addr];\n    stage[6] <= tmem[3'd6][rd_addr];\n    stage[7] <= tmem[3'd7][rd_addr];\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// read side control\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    wr_blk  <= 1'b0;\n    blk_rdy <= 2'b0;\n    rd_blk  <= 1'b0;\n    rd_act  <= 1'b0;\n    rd_cnt  <= 6'd0;\nend\nelse begin\n    if (x_valid && x_cnt == 6'd63) begin\n        wr_blk          <= ~wr_blk;\n        blk_rdy[wr_blk] <= 1'b1;\n    end\n\n    if (rd_act) begin\n        if (rd_cnt == 6'd63) begin\n            rd_act          <= 1'b0;\n            blk_rdy[rd_blk] <= 1'b0;\n            rd_blk          <= ~rd_blk;\n            rd_cnt          <= 6'd0;\n        end\n        else\n            rd_cnt <= rd_cnt + 6'd1;\n    end\n    else if (blk_rdy[rd_blk]) begin\n        rd_act <= 1'b1;\n        rd_cnt <= 6'd0;\n    end\nend\n\n// parity: odd beats {1,3,4,6} take the odd rows\nwire rd_par = ~(rd_beat == 3'd0) & ~(rd_beat == 3'd2) &\n              ~(rd_beat == 3'd5) & ~(rd_beat == 3'd7);\n\nwire signed [MW-1:0] q0 = stage[{2'd0,rd_par}];\nwire signed [MW-1:0] q1 = stage[{2'd1,rd_par}];\nwire signed [MW-1:0] q2 = stage[{2'd2,rd_par}];\nwire signed [MW-1:0] q3 = stage[{2'd3,rd_par}];\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// column pass\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire                y_valid;\nwire [5:0]          y_cnt;\nwire signed [31:0]  y_data;\n\nidct1d #(.IN_W(MW), .OUT_SHIFT(15), .OUT_W(32)) u_y\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.valid_i(rd_valid)\n    ,.idx_i(rd_beat)\n    ,.d0_i(q0)\n    ,.d1_i(q1)\n    ,.d2_i(q2)\n    ,.d3_i(q3)\n    ,.valid_o(y_valid)\n    ,.cnt_o(y_cnt)\n    ,.data_o(y_data)\n);\n\nassign outport_valid_o = y_valid;\nassign outport_data_o  = y_data;\nassign outport_idx_o   = {y_cnt[2:0], y_cnt[5:3]};\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design8.sv')

# Summary

## Final result
**`design5.sv` — PASS (0/20672 fails), cost 4.332e+06 area_delay_product** (delay 1098 ps, area 3945 µm²), vs. the starting point's 9.497e+06 — a **2.19× improvement**.

## 1. Approaches tried

| File | Approach | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `starting_point.v` | Original core_jpeg (4×32-deep dual-port RAMs, 32-bit everywhere) | PASS, 9.50M |
| `design.sv` | Single `idct1d` module reused for both passes; simplified transpose | PASS (after fixes), 16.5M — **worse** |
| `design2.sv` | + registered column-read staging + `/256` via `+255, >>>8` | PASS, 5.49M |
| `design4.sv` | + butterfly restructured so each stage does one add; `sp5/sp6` split out | PASS, 4.61M |
| **`design5.sv`** | + corner turn re-laid out as `tmem[8][16]` (transposed store, 1-D row decode, `MW=20`) | **PASS, 4.33M ← best** |
| `design7.sv` | + registered lane registers (kill parity mux before multiplier) | PASS, 4.73M |
| `design8.sv` | literal-constant multiplies (shift/add networks) | FAIL (timing/ordering bug) |

## 2. Optimizations that mattered most
- **Restructuring the butterfly so no stage chains two 32-bit adds** (4.61M from 5.49M). Key: precompute `o_t5 = (p0-p1)-(p2-p3)` and `o_t6 = o_s7-o_s6` in beat 4, freeing beat 5.
- **Corner-turn memory re-layout**: `tmem[row][{bank,col}]` instead of `tmem[bank][row][col]`. Write address becomes 1-D (cheap), read decode is 8×16 instead of 2×8×8 — this is what took 4.61M → 4.33M.
- **Registered column staging** (`stage[0:7]` loaded once per column) to keep the RAM mux off the multiplier input path.
- **Cheap signed `/256`**: `(x + (x[31]?255:0)) >>> 8` instead of a real divider.
- Narrowing the corner-turn word to 20 bits (safe: verified against all vectors).

## 3. What didn't work
- **`design.sv`'s single-shared-module** composition: register count ballooned (16.5M) because both passes share a 32-bit datapath.
- **Adding an extra pipeline stage to break the `*181/256` chain** (design3): broke the `o_t3`/`o_t4` lifetimes; the schedule has no slack for it.
- **`i0` must be a *latched* register** (captured on beats 0 and 2), not a wire from the current beat — this was the single biggest functional bug.
- **`ld_col`/`stage_a` must be 0 when idle**, not `rd_col+1` — otherwise the preload reads the wrong column.
- `design7`'s lane registers *increased* area more than they reduced delay.
- `target_delay` sweep: 100 ps and 500 ps gave identical results; 1500 ps gave 4.71M (worse). Not a useful lever here.

## 4. Lessons / next steps
- The **critical path is still the corner-turn RAM read mux → column pass multiplier** (1098 ps). Next idea: keep the column pass but replace the 8×16 read mux with a **shift-register column buffer** (serially shift in rows as they're written, no address decode at all) — that should remove the mux entirely.
- `design8.sv`'s literal-constant approach is likely a real win once fixed; the bug is in the lane-select `if/else` chain (beats 3,4,6 map `d2*C3` and `d1*C5`, but the `odd_b` grouping is applied to *both* p2 and p3 while p0/p1 have separate cases — re-derive the truth table from `starting_point.v` lines ~200–290).
- Budget: ~12 of 60 steps were spent on functional debugging of the `i0` latch and the stage-preload address. Reading the golden `jpeg_idct_x` index schedule *line by line* against my own before writing would have saved most of that.
- Keep `design5.sv` as the fallback; every experiment should be a new file.
